### cloudfront-faq-lambda-edge-four-trigger-events | capability hard=True rel=core vol=stable tier=2 rev=1
  C: Lambda@Edge functions trigger automatically on four CloudFront events: viewer request, viewer response, origin request and origin response. Origin request runs when the edge does not have the requested object in its cache; origin response runs when the edge receives the origin's response.
  D: ['Viewer Request', 'Origin Request', 'Origin Response']  S: Lambda@Edge triggers on CloudFront events||False|
  Q[faqs]: Your Lambda@Edge functions will automatically trigger in response to the following Amazon CloudFront events:
  Q[faqs]: Viewer Request: This event occurs when an end user or a device on the Internet makes an HTTP(S) request to CloudFront, and the request arrives at the edge location closest to that user.
  Q[faqs]: Viewer Response: This event occurs when the CloudFront server at the edge is ready to respond to the end user or the device that made the request.
  Q[faqs]: Origin Request: This event occurs when the CloudFront edge server does not already have the requested object in its cache, and the viewer request is ready to be sent to your backend origin webserver (e.g. Amazon EC2, or Application Load Balancer, or Amazon S3)
  Q[faqs]: Origin Response: This event occurs when the CloudFront server at the edge receives a response from your backend origin webserver.
